<body>
    <div id="viewport"></div>
    
    <div id="stats-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>STATISTICS</span>
            <button class="arrow-btn" onclick="togglePanel(this)">▼</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="stats-content">Loading...</div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="search-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>SEARCH</span>
            <button class="arrow-btn" onclick="togglePanel(this)">▼</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <input type="text" id="search-input" placeholder="Search nodes...">
            <div id="search-results"></div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="controls-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span>CONTROLS</span>
            <button class="arrow-btn" onclick="togglePanel(this)">▼</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content">
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">APPEARANCE</div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link width</span>
                        <span id="width-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.65</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.65">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Link opacity</span>
                        <span id="opacity-value" style="color: #667eea;">0.45</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="opacity-slider" min="0" max="1" step="0.01" value="0.45">
                </div>
                <div style="margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                        <span>Stars count</span>
                        <span id="stars-value" style="color: #667eea;">2000</span>
                    </div>
                    <input type="range" id="stars-slider" min="0" max="4000" step="100" value="2000">
                </div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="control-section">
                <div class="control-title">COLORS</div>
                <div id="color-controls"></div>
                <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; margin-top: 10px;">
                    <button onclick="randomizeColors()">🎲 Randomize</button>
                    <button onclick="resetColors()">↺ Reset</button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="node-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="panel-header">
            <span id="node-title">Node Info</span>
            <button class="arrow-btn" onclick="togglePanel(this)">▼</button>
        </div>
        <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">Select a node to view details</div>
    </div>
    
    <div id="bottom-panel" class="panel">
        <div class="status-item">
            <span class="status-dot"></span>
            <span>Connected</span>
        </div>
        <div class="status-item">
            <span>Updated: <span id="timestamp"></span></span>
        </div>
        <button id="rotate-btn" class="icon-btn" style="font-size:10pt;">
            <span style="font-size:10pt; color:#4a90e2;">⏸</span>
            <span>Stop</span>
        </button>
    </div>

    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>
    
    <script src="js/config.js"></script>
    <script src="js/utils.js"></script>
    <script src="js/colorManager.js"></script>
    <script src="js/graphCore.js"></script>
    <script src="js/panelManager.js"></script>
    <script src="js/fix-graph.js"></script>

    <script>
    // ========== ЕДИНСТВЕННАЯ ФУНКЦИЯ ДЛЯ СВОРАЧИВАНИЯ ==========
    function togglePanel(btn) {
        var header = btn.closest(".panel-header");
        if (!header) return;
        
        header.classList.toggle("collapsed");
        btn.innerHTML = header.classList.contains("collapsed") ? "▶" : "▼";
        
        var panel = header.parentElement;
        if (panel.id === "controls-panel") {
            if (header.classList.contains("collapsed")) {
                var nodePanel = document.getElementById("node-panel");
                if (nodePanel) nodePanel.classList.remove("visible");
            } else {
                setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
            }
            var nodePanel = document.getElementById("node-panel");
            if (nodePanel) nodePanel.classList.remove("visible");
        }
        if (panel.id === "node-panel" && !header.classList.contains("collapsed") && window.PanelManager) {
            setTimeout(function() { PanelManager.positionNodePanel(); }, 50);
        }
    }
    
    // ========== ГЛОБАЛЬНЫЕ ПЕРЕМЕННЫЕ ==========
    let graph;
    let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
    let colors = {
        'Person': '#3366ff',
        'Technology': '#33cc33',
        'Component': '#ff9933',
        'Decision': '#cc33ff',
        'Chunk': '#292929'
    };
    let rotationEnabled = true;
    let lastInteraction = Date.now();
    let selectedNodeId = null;
    let fixedRadius = null;
    let rotationAngle = 0;
    let starsCount = 2000;
    let starsMesh = null;
    let isDraggingGraph = false;
    
    // ========== ЗАГРУЗКА ДАННЫХ ==========
    fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/decisions?limit=200')
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            if (!data.nodes || !data.links) return;
            
            const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                const group = node.label || node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
                const connections = data.links.filter(e => 
                    e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
                ).length;
                
                let val;
                if (group === 'Chunk') {
                    val = Math.max(2, Math.min(5, Math.ceil(connections / 15)));
                } else {
                    val = Math.max(3, Math.min(8, Math.ceil(connections / 5)));
                }
                
                if (!val) val = 3;
                return {
                    id: String(node.id),
                    name: node.name || node.label || String(node.id),
                    group: group,
                    val: val
                };
            });
            
            const links = data.links.map(edge => ({
                source: String(edge.source),
                target: String(edge.target),
                type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
            }));
            
            const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
                nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
            );
            
            graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
            initGraph();
            updateStats();
            initColorControls();
        });
    
    // ========== ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ ГРАФА ==========
    function initGraph() {
        graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.getElementById('viewport'))
            .graphData(graphData)
            .nodeId('id')
            .nodeLabel('name')
            .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
            .nodeVal('val')
            .linkWidth(0.65)
            .linkOpacity(0.45)
            .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
            .onNodeHover(node => {
                document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
                if (!selectedNodeId && !isDraggingGraph) highlightNode(node);
            })
            .onNodeClick(node => {
                if (!isDraggingGraph) {
                    selectedNodeId = node.id;
                    showNodeInfo(node);
                    highlightNode(node, true);
                    
                    const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
                    if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                        controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
                    }
                    
                    setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
                }
            })
            .onBackgroundClick(() => {
                if (selectedNodeId) {
                    closeNodePanel();
                }
            });
        
        const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
        if (canvas) {
            canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => { isDraggingGraph = true; });
            canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => { isDraggingGraph = false; });
            canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { isDraggingGraph = false; });
        }
        
        graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
        addStars(starsCount);
        setupControls();
        setupAutoRotate();
    }
    
    // ========== ПОЗИЦИОНИРОВАНИЕ ПАНЕЛИ ИНФО ==========
    function positionNodePanel() {
        const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
        const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
        
        if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel || !nodePanel.classList.contains('visible')) return;
        
        const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsPanel.offsetHeight;
        nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
    }
    
    // ========== ПОДСВЕТКА УЗЛОВ ==========
    function highlightNode(node, permanent = false) {
        if (!graph) return;
        
        if (node) {
            const nodeId = node.id;
            const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
                (l.source.id || l.source) === nodeId || 
                (l.target.id || l.target) === nodeId
            );
            
            const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
            connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id || l.source);
                connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id || l.target);
            });
            
            graph.nodeColor(n => {
                if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                    return colors[n.group] || '#888';
                }
                return '#333';
            });
            
            graph.linkColor(l => {
                if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
                return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
            });
            
            graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : 0.65);
        } else if (!permanent) {
            graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
                 .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
                 .linkWidth(0.65);
        }
    }
    
    // ========== ПАНЕЛЬ ИНФОРМАЦИИ ОБ УЗЛЕ ==========
    function showNodeInfo(node) {
        const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
        const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
        const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
        
        panel.classList.add('visible');
        title.textContent = node.name || node.id;
        
        const connections = [];
        graphData.links.forEach(link => {
            const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
            const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
            
            if (sourceId === node.id) {
                const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                connections.push({
                    type: link.type || 'related',
                    with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                    direction: '→',
                    targetId: targetId
                });
            }
            if (targetId === node.id) {
                const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                connections.push({
                    type: link.type || 'related',
                    with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                    direction: '←',
                    targetId: sourceId
                });
            }
        });
        
        let html = `
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">TYPE</div>
                <div>${node.group || 'Unknown'}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">ID</div>
                <div>${node.id}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="node-property">
                <div class="label">CONNECTIONS</div>
                <div>${connections.length}</div>
            </div>
        `;
        
        if (connections.length > 0) {
            connections.forEach(conn => {
                html += `
                    <div class="connection-item" onclick="focusNode('${conn.targetId}')">
                        ${conn.type} ${conn.direction} ${conn.with}
                    </div>
                `;
            });
        }
        
        content.innerHTML = html;
    }
    
    function closeNodePanel() {
        document.getElementById('node-panel').classList.remove('visible');
        selectedNodeId = null;
        highlightNode(null);
    }
    
    function focusNode(nodeId) {
        const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
        if (node) {
            selectedNodeId = nodeId;
            showNodeInfo(node);
            highlightNode(node, true);
            
            const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#controls-panel .panel-header');
            if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
            }
            
            setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 300);
            
            if (graph) {
                graph.cameraPosition(
                    { x: 80, y: 40, z: 80 },
                    node,
                    800
                );
            }
        }
    }
    
    // ========== СТАТИСТИКА ==========
    function updateStats() {
        const groups = {};
        graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
        
        let html = `
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">Total nodes:</span>
                <span class="stat-value">${graphData.nodes.length}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="stat-row">
                <span class="stat-label">Total edges:</span>
                <span class="stat-value">${graphData.links.length}</span>
            </div>
        `;
        
        for (let [group, count] of Object.entries(groups)) {
            html += `
                <div class="stat-row">
                    <span class="stat-label">${group}:</span>
                    <span class="stat-value">${count}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }
        
        document.getElementById('stats-content').innerHTML = html;
        document.getElementById('timestamp').textContent = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
    }
    
    // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ЦВЕТАМИ ==========
    function initColorControls() {
        const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
        if (!container) return;
        
        const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
        let html = '';
        
        groups.forEach(group => {
            if (group !== 'unknown') {
                const color = colors[group] || (group === 'Chunk' ? '#292929' : '#888');
                html += `
                    <div class="color-control">
                        <span class="color-label">${group}:</span>
                        <input type="color" class="color-input" value="${color}" 
                               onchange="updateGroupColor('${group}', this.value)">
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        });
        
        container.innerHTML = html;
    }
    
    function updateGroupColor(group, color) {
        colors[group] = color;
        if (graph) {
            graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
            if (selectedNodeId) {
                const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
            }
        }
    }
    
    function randomizeColors() {
        const groups = Object.keys(colors);
        groups.forEach(group => {
            const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
            colors[group] = `hsl(${hue}, 85%, 60%)`;
        });
        initColorControls();
        if (graph) {
            graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
            if (selectedNodeId) {
                const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
            }
        }
    }
    
    function resetColors() {
        colors = {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#292929'
        };
        initColorControls();
        if (graph) {
            graph.nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888');
            if (selectedNodeId) {
                const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === selectedNodeId);
                if (node) highlightNode(node, true);
            }
        }
    }
    
    // ========== УПРАВЛЕНИЕ ==========
    function setupControls() {
        document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const val = e.target.value;
            document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
            if (graph) graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
        });
        
        document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const val = e.target.value;
            document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
            if (graph) graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
        });
        
        document.getElementById('stars-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
            document.getElementById('stars-value').textContent = val;
            starsCount = val;
            updateStars(val);
        });
        
        document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
            const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
            btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? '<span style="font-size:10pt; color:#4a90e2;">⏸</span><span>Stop</span>' : '<span style="font-size:10pt; color:#4a90e2;">▶</span><span>Start</span>';
            
            if (rotationEnabled) {
                const camera = graph.camera();
                fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                    camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                    camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                    camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                );
                rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
            }
        });
        
        document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
            const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
            if (!query) {
                document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = '';
                return;
            }
            
            const results = graphData.nodes
                .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
                .slice(0, 5);
            
            document.getElementById('search-results').innerHTML = results
                .map(r => `<button onclick="focusNode('${r.id}')">${r.name}</button>`)
                .join('');
        });
    }
    
    // ========== АВТО-ВРАЩЕНИЕ ==========
    function setupAutoRotate() {
        let lastTime = Date.now();
        
        function rotateCamera() {
            if (!graph) return;
            
            const now = Date.now();
            const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
            lastTime = now;
            
            if (rotationEnabled && !isDraggingGraph && now - lastInteraction > 5000) {
                if (!fixedRadius) {
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                        camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                        camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                        camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                    );
                    rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                }
                
                rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                const camera = graph.camera();
                camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
            } else {
                fixedRadius = null;
            }
            
            requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
        }
        
        const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
        if (canvas) {
            canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                lastInteraction = Date.now();
                isDraggingGraph = true;
            });
            canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
                isDraggingGraph = false;
            });
            canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => lastInteraction = Date.now());
        }
        
        rotateCamera();
    }
    
    // ========== ЗВЕЗДЫ ==========
    function addStars(count) {
        const scene = graph.scene();
        if (!scene) return;
        
        if (starsMesh) {
            scene.remove(starsMesh);
        }
        
        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        const colors_array = new Float32Array(count * 3);
        
        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            positions[i*3] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
            positions[i*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
            positions[i*3+2] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 800;
            
            const brightness = 0.7 + Math.random() * 0.3;
            colors_array[i*3] = brightness;
            colors_array[i*3+1] = brightness * (0.8 + Math.random() * 0.2);
            colors_array[i*3+2] = brightness;
        }
        
        geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
        geometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors_array, 3));
        
        const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
            size: 0.3,
            vertexColors: true,
            transparent: true,
            opacity: 0.8,
            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
        });
        
        starsMesh = new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
        scene.add(starsMesh);
    }
    
    function updateStars(count) {
        addStars(count);
    }
    
    // ========== DRAGGABLE PANELS ==========
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
        let panel = header.parentElement;
        let isDragging = false;
        let offsetX, offsetY;
        
        header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
            if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow-btn')) {
                isDragging = true;
                offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                e.preventDefault();
            }
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if (isDragging) {
                panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                
                if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                    setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
                }
            }
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
            isDragging = false;
        });
    });
    
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
        positionNodePanel();
    });
    
    // ========== ИНИЦИАЛИЗАЦИЯ МОДУЛЕЙ ==========
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
        setTimeout(function() {
            if (typeof ColorManager !== "undefined" && graphData && graphData.nodes) {
                ColorManager.renderControls();
            }
            if (typeof PanelManager !== "undefined") {
                PanelManager.init();
            }
        }, 2000);
    });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

---

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

**ОТЛИЧНО!** Теперь я вижу все параметры из рабочей версии:

## 📊 **ПАРАМЕТРЫ ИЗ РАБОЧЕЙ ВЕРСИИ:**

1. **Физика** - в рабочей версии **НЕТ** явных вызовов `d3Force`! Она использует физику по умолчанию из ForceGraph3D.

2. **Цвета** - хранятся в объекте `colors` и применяются через:
   ```javascript
   .nodeColor(n => colors[n.group] || '#888')
   ```

3. **Инициализация** - данные загружаются, потом вызывается `initGraph()`

## 🔧 **ЧТО НУЖНО ПЕРЕНЕСТИ В МОДУЛЬНУЮ ВЕРСИЮ:**

Теперь я понимаю - в модульной версии мы сами настраиваем физику, а в рабочей - нет. Давайте скопируем поведение рабочей версии:

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public && \

# Обновляем graphCore.js - УБИРАЕМ явную настройку физики
cat > js/graphCore.js << 'EOF'
console.log("%c🚀 graphCore.js MODULAR (with working physics)", "font-size: 20px; color: #4CAF50; font-weight: bold");
console.log("📊 Время загрузки:", new Date().toISOString());

function cleanName(name) {
    if (!name) return 'unknown';
    return String(name).split('|')[0].substring(0, 30).trim() || 'unknown';
}

window.GraphCore = {
    graph: null,
    data: { nodes: [], links: [] },
    selectedNodeId: null,
    isDragging: false,
    
    init: function(container) {
        console.log("🔄 INIT, container:", container);
        this.container = container;
        this.loadData();
    },
    
    createGraph: function() {
        console.log("🔄 Создание графа с данными:", this.data.nodes.length, "узлов");
        
        this.graph = ForceGraph3D()(this.container)
            .graphData(this.data)
            .nodeId('id')
            .nodeLabel('name')
            .nodeColor(n => {
                const color = window.ColorManager ? window.ColorManager.getColor(n.group) : "#888";
                console.log(`🎨 Узел ${n.name} (${n.group}): цвет ${color}`);
                return color;
            })
            .nodeVal('val')
            .linkWidth(0.65)
            .linkOpacity(0.45)
            .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
            .onNodeHover(node => this.onNodeHover(node))
            .onNodeClick(node => this.onNodeClick(node))
            .onBackgroundClick(() => this.onBackgroundClick())
            .cooldownTicks(50)
            .cooldownTime(2000);
        
        // НЕ НАСТРАИВАЕМ физику явно - используем значения по умолчанию как в рабочей версии
        
        this.graph.warmupTicks(100).cooldownTicks(50);
        
        setTimeout(() => {
            this.graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
        }, 500);
        
        console.log("✅ Граф создан");
        
        if (window.ColorManager) window.ColorManager.renderControls();
    },
    
    loadData: async function() {
        console.log("📥 Загрузка данных...");
        
        try {
            const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/decisions?limit=500');
            const data = await res.json();
            
            console.log("📦 Данные получены:", data.nodes?.length || 0, "узлов");
            
            if (!data.nodes) {
                console.log("❌ Нет данных");
                return;
            }
            
            const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                const group = node.label || 'unknown';
                const name = cleanName(node.name || node.label || node.id);
                const connections = data.links ? data.links.filter(e => 
                    e.source === node.id || e.target === node.id
                ).length : 0;
                
                let val;
                if (group === 'Chunk') {
                    val = Math.max(2, Math.min(5, Math.ceil(connections / 15)));
                } else {
                    val = Math.max(3, Math.min(8, Math.ceil(connections / 5)));
                }
                
                return {
                    id: String(node.id),
                    name: name,
                    group: group,
                    val: val || 3
                };
            });
            
            const links = data.links ? data.links.map(edge => ({
                source: String(edge.source),
                target: String(edge.target),
                type: edge.type || 'related'
            })) : [];
            
            const validLinks = links.filter(link => 
                nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source) && 
                nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target)
            );
            
            this.data = { nodes, links: validLinks };
            
            console.log("📊 Итог:", nodes.length, "узлов,", validLinks.length, "связей");
            
            this.createGraph();
            
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("❌ Ошибка:", e);
        }
    },
    
    onNodeHover: function(node) {
        document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
    },
    
    onNodeClick: function(node) {
        console.log("👆 Клик:", node.name);
        this.selectedNodeId = node.id;
        if (window.PanelManager) {
            window.PanelManager.showNodeInfo(node);
        }
    },
    
    onBackgroundClick: function() {
        if (this.selectedNodeId && window.PanelManager) {
            window.PanelManager.hideNodePanel();
            this.selectedNodeId = null;
        }
    },
    
    updateLinkWidth: function(val) {
        if (this.graph) this.graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
    },
    
    updateLinkOpacity: function(val) {
        if (this.graph) this.graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
    },
    
    focusNode: function(nodeId) {
        const node = this.data.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
        if (node && this.graph) {
            this.selectedNodeId = node.id;
            if (window.PanelManager) {
                window.PanelManager.showNodeInfo(node);
            }
            
            const nodeObj = this.graph.graphData().nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (nodeObj && nodeObj.x) {
                this.graph.cameraPosition(
                    { x: nodeObj.x + 50, y: nodeObj.y + 25, z: nodeObj.z + 50 },
                    { x: nodeObj.x, y: nodeObj.y, z: nodeObj.z },
                    800
                );
            }
        }
    }
};

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log("📄 DOM загружен");
    const container = document.getElementById('3d-graph');
    if (container) {
        window.GraphCore.init(container);
    }
});
EOF

echo "✅ graphCore.js обновлен - физика теперь как в рабочей версии"
echo "🔄 Перезагрузите страницу"
```

2. Физика теперь работает как в рабочей версии (по умолчанию)
3. Расчет размера узлов скопирован из рабочей версии

### **ШАГ 2: Добавляем функционал сворачивания панелей**

```bash
cat >> blender_hybrid.modular.html << 'EOF'

<script>
// Функция сворачивания как в рабочей версии
window.togglePanel = function(btn) {
    var header = btn.closest(".panel-header");
    if (!header) return;
    
    header.classList.toggle("collapsed");
    btn.innerHTML = header.classList.contains("collapsed") ? "▶" : "▼";
    
    var panel = header.parentElement;
    if (panel.id === "controls-panel") {
        if (header.classList.contains("collapsed")) {
            var nodePanel = document.getElementById("node-panel");
            if (nodePanel) nodePanel.classList.remove("visible");
        } else {
            setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
        }
    }
    if (panel.id === "node-panel" && !header.classList.contains("collapsed") && window.PanelManager) {
        setTimeout(function() { PanelManager.positionNodePanel(); }, 50);
    }
};

// Функция позиционирования панели узла
window.positionNodePanel = function() {
    const controlsPanel = document.getElementById('controls-panel');
    const nodePanel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
    
    if (!controlsPanel || !nodePanel || !nodePanel.classList.contains('visible')) return;
    
    const controlsHeight = controlsPanel.classList.contains('collapsed') ? 35 : controlsPanel.offsetHeight;
    nodePanel.style.top = (controlsPanel.offsetTop + controlsHeight + 5) + 'px';
};

// Перетаскивание панелей
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    document.querySelectorAll('.panel-header').forEach(header => {
        let panel = header.parentElement;
        let isDragging = false;
        let offsetX, offsetY;
        
        header.addEventListener('mousedown', (e) => {
            if (e.target === header || e.target.classList.contains('arrow-btn')) {
                isDragging = true;
                offsetX = e.clientX - panel.offsetLeft;
                offsetY = e.clientY - panel.offsetTop;
                e.preventDefault();
            }
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
            if (isDragging) {
                panel.style.left = (e.clientX - offsetX) + 'px';
                panel.style.top = (e.clientY - offsetY) + 'px';
                
                if (panel.id === 'controls-panel') {
                    setTimeout(positionNodePanel, 50);
                }
            }
        });
        
        document.addEventListener('mouseup', () => {
            isDragging = false;
        });
    });
});
</script>

### **ШАГ 3: Добавляем управление (слайдеры, поиск, вращение)**

```bash
cat >> blender_hybrid.modular.html << 'EOF'

<script>
// Управление
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    // Слайдеры
    document.getElementById('width-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        const val = e.target.value;
        document.getElementById('width-value').textContent = val;
        if (window.GraphCore) window.GraphCore.updateLinkWidth(val);
    });
    
    document.getElementById('opacity-slider').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        const val = e.target.value;
        document.getElementById('opacity-value').textContent = val;
        if (window.GraphCore) window.GraphCore.updateLinkOpacity(val);
    });
    
    // Вращение
    let rotationEnabled = true;
    let lastInteraction = Date.now();
    let isDraggingGraph = false;
    let fixedRadius = null;
    let rotationAngle = 0;
    
    document.getElementById('rotate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
        rotationEnabled = !rotationEnabled;
        const btn = document.getElementById('rotate-btn');
        btn.innerHTML = rotationEnabled ? 
            '<span style="color:#4a90e2;">⏸</span><span>Stop</span>' : 
            '<span style="color:#4a90e2;">▶</span><span>Start</span>';
    });
    
    // Автовращение
    function setupAutoRotate() {
        let lastTime = Date.now();
        
        function rotateCamera() {
            if (!window.GraphCore || !window.GraphCore.graph) return;
            
            const now = Date.now();
            const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
            lastTime = now;
            
            if (rotationEnabled && !isDraggingGraph && now - lastInteraction > 5000) {
                const camera = window.GraphCore.graph.camera();
                if (!fixedRadius) {
                    fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                        camera.position.x ** 2 +
                        camera.position.y ** 2 +
                        camera.position.z ** 2
                    );
                    rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                }
                
                rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
            } else {
                fixedRadius = null;
            }
            
            requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
        }
        
        rotateCamera();
    }
    
    // Поиск
    document.getElementById('search-input').addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        const query = e.target.value.toLowerCase();
        const results = document.getElementById('search-results');
        
        if (!query || !window.GraphCore || !window.GraphCore.data) {
            results.innerHTML = '';
            return;
        }
        
        const matches = window.GraphCore.data.nodes
            .filter(n => n.name.toLowerCase().includes(query))
            .slice(0, 5);
        
        results.innerHTML = matches
            .map(n => `<button onclick="window.GraphCore.focusNode('${n.id}')">${n.name}</button>`)
            .join('');
    });
    
    // Запускаем автовращение после загрузки графа
    setTimeout(setupAutoRotate, 2000);
});
</script>
EOF

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public && \

# Создаем новый HTML на основе рабочей версии

' blender_hybrid.modular.new.html && \

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <title>3D Knowledge Graph</title>
    <style>
        body { margin: 0; overflow: hidden; font-family: 'Segoe UI', Arial, sans-serif; background-color: #0a0a1a; }
        
        /* Контейнер для правых панелей */
        #right-panels {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            right: 20px;
            width: 320px;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            gap: 10px;
            z-index: 10000;
            pointer-events: none;
        }
        
        /* Стили для панелей */
        .graph-panel {
            background: rgba(15, 17, 22, 0.85);
            color: rgba(220, 220, 240, 0.9);
            backdrop-filter: blur(8px);
            border: 1px solid rgba(68, 102, 170, 0.3);
            border-radius: 8px;
            width: 100%;
            pointer-events: auto;
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
        }
        
        .graph-panel.info {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px;
            left: 20px;
            width: auto;
            pointer-events: none;
            background: rgba(15, 17, 22, 0.75);
        }
        
        .graph-panel.stats {
            position: absolute;
            bottom: 20px;
            left: 20px;
            width: 280px;
            pointer-events: auto;
        }
        
        .panel-header {
            padding: 12px 16px;
            cursor: pointer;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(68, 102, 170, 0.2);
            user-select: none;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
        }
        
        .panel-header:hover {
            background: rgba(68, 102, 170, 0.15);
        }
        
        .panel-header .arrow {
            transition: transform 0.3s;
            font-size: 12px;
            color: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.8);
        }
        
        .panel-header.collapsed .arrow {
            transform: rotate(-90deg);
        }
        
        .panel-content {
            padding: 16px;
            transition: max-height 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease, padding 0.3s ease;
            max-height: 600px;
            opacity: 1;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        
        .panel-header.collapsed + .panel-content {
            max-height: 0;
            opacity: 0;
            padding: 0 16px;
        }
        
        /* Панель информации об узле - С ПРОКРУТКОЙ */
        #node-panel {
            margin-top: 0;
            max-height: 70vh;  /* Ограничиваем максимальную высоту */
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
        }
        
        #node-panel.hidden {
            display: none;
        }
        
        #node-panel .panel-header {
            flex-shrink: 0;  /* Заголовок не сжимается */
        }
        
        #node-panel .panel-content {
            overflow-y: auto;  /* Включаем прокрутку */
            max-height: calc(70vh - 50px);  /* Высота с учетом заголовка */
            padding: 16px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .close-btn {
            position: absolute;
            top: 12px;
            right: 12px;
            cursor: pointer;
            color: rgba(255,255,255,0.5);
            font-size: 18px;
            background: none;
            border: none;
            width: 24px;
            height: 24px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
            border-radius: 50%;
            z-index: 10;
        }
        #node-panel .close-btn:hover {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            color: rgba(255,255,255,0.9);
        }
        
        #node-panel h3 {
            margin: 0 0 16px 0;
            color: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.9);
            font-size: 16px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(68, 102, 170, 0.3);
            padding-bottom: 10px;
            padding-right: 24px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .property {
            margin: 10px 0;
            padding: 8px 12px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            border-radius: 6px;
            border-left: 2px solid rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.5);
        }
        
        #node-panel .property .label {
            color: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.8);
            margin-right: 8px;
            font-size: 12px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .connections { 
            margin-top: 16px; 
        }
        
        #node-panel .connections h4 {
            color: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.8);
            font-size: 14px;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .connection-item {
            padding: 8px 12px;
            margin: 4px 0;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.02);
            border-radius: 4px;
            border-left: 2px solid rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.5);
            font-size: 12px;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: background 0.2s;
        }
        #node-panel .connection-item:hover {
            background: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.15);
        }
        
        #node-panel .connection-type { 
            color: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.8); 
            margin-right: 4px;
        }
        
        .stat-row {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            margin: 6px 0;
            padding: 4px 0;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }
        .stat-label { color: rgba(136, 170, 255, 0.8); }
        .stat-value { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.9); font-weight: 400; }

        /* Стили для контролов */
        .control-content input[type=range] {
            -webkit-appearance: none;
            background: transparent;
            width: 100%;
        }
        .control-content input[type=range]::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {
            width: 100%;
            height: 4px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            border-radius: 2px;
        }
        .control-content input[type=range]::-webkit-slider-thumb {
            -webkit-appearance: none;
            width: 16px;
            height: 16px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: rgba(136,170,255,0.8);
            margin-top: -6px;
            cursor: pointer;
        }
        .control-content .color-input {
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            margin-bottom: 8px;
            gap: 8px;
        }
        .control-content .color-input span {
            width: 80px;
            color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);
            font-size: 12px;
        }
        .control-content .color-input input {
            flex: 1;
            height: 25px;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            border: 1px solid rgba(136,170,255,0.3);
            border-radius: 4px;
            cursor: pointer;
        }
        .control-content button {
            background: rgba(68,102,170,0.3);
            border: 1px solid rgba(136,170,255,0.3);
            color: white;
            padding: 8px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: background 0.2s;
        }
        .control-content button:hover {
            background: rgba(68,102,170,0.5);
        }
        
        /* Стилизация скроллбара */
        #node-panel .panel-content::-webkit-scrollbar {
            width: 6px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .panel-content::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
            border-radius: 3px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .panel-content::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
            background: rgba(136,170,255,0.3);
            border-radius: 3px;
        }
        
        #node-panel .panel-content::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
            background: rgba(136,170,255,0.5);
        }
    </style>
</head>
<body>
    <!-- Библиотеки -->
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/d3/7.8.5/d3.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/three@0.128.0/build/three.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/3d-force-graph@1.74.5/dist/3d-force-graph.min.js"></script>

    <script>
        // Глобальные переменные
        let graph;
        let graphData = { nodes: [], links: [] };
        let lastInteraction = Date.now();
        let fixedRadius = null;
        let rotationAngle = 0;
        let isRotating = false;
        
        // Цвета для разных типов узлов
        const colors = {
            'Person': '#3366ff',
            'Technology': '#33cc33',
            'Component': '#ff9933',
            'Task': '#ffcc00',
            'Decision': '#cc33ff',
            'Chunk': '#666666'
        };
        
        function getNodeColor(group) {
            if (group === 'Chunk') return '#555555';
            return colors[group] || '#888888';
        }
        
        // Функция создания панелей
        function createPanels() {
            console.log('Создаем панели...');
            
            document.querySelectorAll('.graph-panel, #right-panels').forEach(p => p.remove());
            
            // Info панель (слева сверху)
            const info = document.createElement('div');
            info.className = 'graph-panel info';
            info.id = 'info';
            info.textContent = '📊 UCH KNOWLEDGE GRAPH';
            document.body.appendChild(info);
            
            // Stats панель (слева снизу)
            const stats = document.createElement('div');
            stats.className = 'graph-panel stats';
            stats.id = 'stats';
            stats.innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel-header" onclick="this.classList.toggle('collapsed')">
                    <span style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);">📊 СТАТИСТИКА</span>
                    <span class="arrow">▼</span>
                </div>
                <div class="panel-content" id="stats-content">
                    Загрузка...
                </div>
            `;
            document.body.appendChild(stats);
            
            // Контейнер для правых панелей
            const rightPanels = document.createElement('div');
            rightPanels.id = 'right-panels';
            document.body.appendChild(rightPanels);
            
            // Control панель
            const control = document.createElement('div');
            control.className = 'graph-panel';
            control.id = 'control-panel';
            control.innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel-header" onclick="toggleControlPanel(this)">
                    <span style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);">🎮 УПРАВЛЕНИЕ</span>
                    <span class="arrow">▼</span>
                </div>
                <div class="panel-content control-content">
                    <!-- Цвета групп -->
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
                        <div style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.7); margin-bottom: 10px; font-size: 13px;">🎨 ЦВЕТА ГРУПП</div>
                        <div id="color-controls"></div>
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- Ползунки -->
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 15px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                            <span style="color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);">📏 Толщина связей</span>
                            <span id="link-width-value" style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);">0.25</span>
                        </div>
                        <input type="range" id="link-width-slider" min="0" max="2" step="0.05" value="0.25">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 15px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                            <span style="color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);">✨ Яркость связей</span>
                            <span id="link-opacity-value" style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);">0.15</span>
                        </div>
                        <input type="range" id="link-opacity-slider" min="0" max="0.5" step="0.01" value="0.15">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 15px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                            <span style="color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);">⚡ Сила отталкивания</span>
                            <span id="charge-value" style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);">-120</span>
                        </div>
                        <input type="range" id="charge-slider" min="-300" max="-30" step="10" value="-120">
                    </div>
                    
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 20px;">
                        <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
                            <span style="color: rgba(255,255,255,0.7);">📐 Длина связей</span>
                            <span id="distance-value" style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);">1.0</span>
                        </div>
                        <input type="range" id="distance-slider" min="0.5" max="2" step="0.1" value="1.0">
                    </div>
                    
                    <!-- Кнопки -->
                    <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px;">
                        <button onclick="randomizePositions()">🎲 Перемешать</button>
                        <button onclick="resetToDefault()">↺ Сброс</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
            rightPanels.appendChild(control);
            
            // Панель информации об узле - С ПРОКРУТКОЙ
            const nodePanel = document.createElement('div');
            nodePanel.className = 'graph-panel hidden';
            nodePanel.id = 'node-panel';
            nodePanel.innerHTML = `
                <div class="panel-header">
                    <span style="color: rgba(136,170,255,0.9);" id="node-title">Информация об узле</span>
                    <button class="close-btn" onclick="closeNodePanel()">✖</button>
                </div>
                <div class="panel-content" id="node-content">
                    Выберите узел для просмотра информации
                </div>
            `;
            rightPanels.appendChild(nodePanel);
            
            console.log('Панели созданы');
        }
        
        function toggleControlPanel(header) {
            header.classList.toggle('collapsed');
            // Если открываем управление, закрываем панель узла
            if (!header.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                closeNodePanel();
            }
        }
        
        function closeNodePanel() {
            const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            if (panel) {
                panel.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        }
        
        function showNodeInfo(node) {
            const panel = document.getElementById('node-panel');
            const title = document.getElementById('node-title');
            const content = document.getElementById('node-content');
            const controlHeader = document.querySelector('#control-panel .panel-header');
            
            if (!panel || !title || !content) return;
            
            // Сворачиваем панель управления
            if (controlHeader && !controlHeader.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                controlHeader.classList.add('collapsed');
            }
            
            // Показываем панель узла
            panel.classList.remove('hidden');
            title.textContent = `🔍 ${node.name || node.id || 'Unknown'}`;
            
            const nodeId = node.id;
            const connections = [];
            
            graphData.links.forEach(link => {
                const sourceId = typeof link.source === 'object' ? link.source.id : link.source;
                const targetId = typeof link.target === 'object' ? link.target.id : link.target;
                
                if (sourceId === nodeId) {
                    const target = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === targetId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: target ? (target.name || target.id) : targetId,
                        direction: 'out',
                        targetId: targetId
                    });
                }
                if (targetId === nodeId) {
                    const source = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === sourceId);
                    connections.push({
                        type: link.type || 'related',
                        with: source ? (source.name || source.id) : sourceId,
                        direction: 'in',
                        targetId: sourceId
                    });
                }
            });
            
            let html = `
                <div class="property">
                    <span class="label">📋 Тип:</span> ${node.group || 'Unknown'}
                </div>
                <div class="property">
                    <span class="label">🔖 ID:</span> ${node.id || 'N/A'}
                </div>
            `;
            
            if (connections.length > 0) {
                html += '<div class="connections"><h4>🔗 СВЯЗИ (' + connections.length + ')</h4>';
                connections.forEach(conn => {
                    const arrow = conn.direction === 'out' ? '→' : '←';
                    html += `
                        <div class="connection-item" onclick="focusNode('${conn.targetId}')">
                            <span class="connection-type">${conn.type}</span> ${arrow} 
                            <span style="color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8);">${conn.with}</span>
                        </div>
                    `;
                });
                html += '</div>';
            }
            
            content.innerHTML = html;
            // Прокручиваем в начало
            content.scrollTop = 0;
        }

        function focusNode(nodeId) {
            if (!graph) return;
            
            const node = graphData.nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
            if (node) {
                showNodeInfo(node);
                
                const nodeObj = graph.graphData().nodes.find(n => n.id === nodeId);
                if (nodeObj && nodeObj.x) {
                    graph.cameraPosition(
                        { x: nodeObj.x + 80, y: nodeObj.y + 40, z: nodeObj.z + 80 },
                        { x: nodeObj.x, y: nodeObj.y, z: nodeObj.z },
                        800
                    );
                    
                    const camera = graph.camera();
                    if (camera) {
                        fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                            camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                            camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                            camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                        );
                        rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Загрузка данных
        fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/visualization?limit=200')
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                console.log('Данные получены:', data);
                
                if (!data.nodes || !data.edges) return;
                
                const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => {
                    let group = node.group || node.type || 'unknown';
                    if (node.label && node.label.includes('Chunk')) group = 'Chunk';
                    else if (node.name && node.name.includes('Chunk')) group = 'Chunk';
                    
                    return {
                        id: String(node.id),
                        name: node.label || node.name || String(node.id),
                        group: group
                    };
                });
                
                const links = data.edges.map(edge => ({
                    source: String(edge.source),
                    target: String(edge.target),
                    type: edge.label || edge.type || 'related'
                }));
                
                const validLinks = links.filter(link => {
                    const sourceExists = nodes.some(n => n.id === link.source);
                    const targetExists = nodes.some(n => n.id === link.target);
                    return sourceExists && targetExists;
                });
                
                graphData = { nodes, links: validLinks };
                
                initGraph();
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Ошибка:', error);
            });
        
        function initGraph() {
            try {
                const oldCanvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
                if (oldCanvas) oldCanvas.remove();
                
                // Создаем граф
                graph = ForceGraph3D()(document.body)
                    .graphData(graphData)
                    .nodeId('id')
                    .nodeLabel('name')
                    .nodeVal(node => node.value * 3)
                    .nodeColor(n => getNodeColor(n.group))
                    .nodeRelSize(2.5)
                    .linkWidth(0.25)
                    .linkOpacity(0.15)
                    .linkDirectionalParticles(0)
                    .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)')
                    .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
                    .onNodeHover(node => {
                        document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';

                        window.myGraph = graph;
                        
                        if (node && graph) {
                            const nodeId = node.id;
                            const connectedLinks = graphData.links.filter(l => 
                                l.source.id === nodeId || l.target.id === nodeId
                            );
                            
                            const connectedNodeIds = new Set();
                            connectedLinks.forEach(l => {
                                connectedNodeIds.add(l.source.id);
                                connectedNodeIds.add(l.target.id);
                            });
                            
                            graph.nodeColor(n => {
                                if (n.id === nodeId || connectedNodeIds.has(n.id)) {
                                    return getNodeColor(n.group);
                                }
                                return '#333';
                            });
                            
                            graph.linkColor(l => {
                                if (connectedLinks.includes(l)) return '#ffaa00';
                                return 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)';
                            });
                            
                            graph.linkWidth(l => connectedLinks.includes(l) ? 0.8 : 0.2);
                        } else {
                            graph.nodeColor(n => getNodeColor(n.group))
                                 .linkColor(() => 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)')
                                 .linkWidth(0.25);
                        }
                    })
                    .onNodeClick(node => showNodeInfo(node))
                    .cooldownTime(2000);
                
                // Начальная позиция камеры
                graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
                
                const camera = graph.camera();
                if (camera) {
                    fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                        camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                        camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                        camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                    );
                    rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                }
                
                // Создаем панели
                createPanels();
                
                // Обновляем статистику
                updateStats();
                
                // Инициализируем контролы цветов
                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (graphData.nodes.length > 0) {
                        initColorControls();
                    }
                }, 1000);
                
                // Звезды
                const scene = graph.scene();
                if (scene) {
                    function createStarsWithVoid(count, radius, voidRadius, color, size, opacity) {
                        const geometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
                        const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
                        
                        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                            let x, y, z, dist;
                            do {
                                x = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                                y = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                                z = (Math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 2;
                                dist = Math.sqrt(x*x + y*y + z*z);
                            } while (dist < voidRadius);
                            
                            positions[i*3] = x;
                            positions[i*3+1] = y;
                            positions[i*3+2] = z;
                        }
                        
                        geometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
                        
                        const material = new THREE.PointsMaterial({ 
                            color, size, transparent: true, opacity,
                            blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
                        });
                        
                        return new THREE.Points(geometry, material);
                    }
                    
                    const voidRadius = 120;
                    const stars1 = createStarsWithVoid(8000, 1400, voidRadius, 0xffffff, 0.4, 0.9);
                    scene.add(stars1);
                    
                    const stars2 = createStarsWithVoid(4000, 2200, voidRadius * 1.8, 0xaaccff, 0.2, 0.6);
                    scene.add(stars2);
                    
                    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x0a0a1a, 0.00015);
                }
                
                // Запускаем авто-вращение
                setupAutoRotate();
                
                console.log('Граф инициализирован');
                
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Ошибка:', error);
            }
        }
        
        function updateStats() {
            const groups = {};
            graphData.nodes.forEach(n => groups[n.group] = (groups[n.group] || 0) + 1);
            
            let groupsHtml = '';
            Object.entries(groups).forEach(([group, count]) => {
                groupsHtml += `<div class="stat-row"><span class="stat-label">${group}:</span> <span class="stat-value">${count}</span></div>`;
            });
            
            const statsContent = document.getElementById('stats-content');
            if (statsContent) {
                statsContent.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="stat-row"><span class="stat-label">Всего узлов:</span> <span class="stat-value">${graphData.nodes.length}</span></div>
                    <div class="stat-row"><span class="stat-label">Всего связей:</span> <span class="stat-value">${graphData.links.length}</span></div>
                    <div style="margin-top: 10px; color: rgba(136,170,255,0.7); font-size: 12px;">📊 ПО ТИПАМ:</div>
                    ${groupsHtml}
                `;
            }
        }
        
        // АВТО-ВРАЩЕНИЕ
        function setupAutoRotate() {
            let lastTime = Date.now();
            
            function rotateCamera() {
                if (!graph) return;
                
                const now = Date.now();
                const deltaTime = now - lastTime;
                lastTime = now;
                
                // Проверяем бездействие
                if (now - lastInteraction > 10000) {
                    if (!isRotating) {
                        isRotating = true;
                        
                        // Запоминаем текущий радиус и угол при старте
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        if (camera) {
                            fixedRadius = Math.sqrt(
                                camera.position.x * camera.position.x +
                                camera.position.y * camera.position.y +
                                camera.position.z * camera.position.z
                            );
                            rotationAngle = Math.atan2(camera.position.x, camera.position.z);
                        }
                    }
                    
                    if (isRotating && fixedRadius) {
                        // Плавное вращение
                        rotationAngle += deltaTime * 0.00015;
                        
                        const camera = graph.camera();
                        if (camera) {
                            camera.position.x = fixedRadius * Math.sin(rotationAngle);
                            camera.position.z = fixedRadius * Math.cos(rotationAngle);
                            // Сохраняем текущую высоту
                            camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    if (isRotating) {
                        isRotating = false;
                    }
                }
                
                requestAnimationFrame(rotateCamera);
            }
            
            // Отслеживаем взаимодействие
            const canvas = document.querySelector('canvas');
            if (canvas) {
                canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {
                    lastInteraction = Date.now();
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('wheel', () => {
                    lastInteraction = Date.now();
                });
                canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', (e) => {
                    if (e.buttons === 1 || e.buttons === 2) {
                        lastInteraction = Date.now();
                    }
                });
            }
            
            rotateCamera();
        }
        
        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            if (graph) {
                graph.width(window.innerWidth);
                graph.height(window.innerHeight);
            }
        });

        // Функции для управления
        function updateLinkWidth(val) {
            if (!graph) return;
            document.getElementById('link-width-value').textContent = val;
            graph.linkWidth(parseFloat(val));
        }

        function updateLinkOpacity(val) {
            if (!graph) return;
            document.getElementById('link-opacity-value').textContent = val;
            graph.linkOpacity(parseFloat(val));
        }

        function updateCharge(val) {
            if (!graph) return;
            document.getElementById('charge-value').textContent = val;
            graph.d3Force('charge', d3.forceManyBody().strength(parseInt(val)));
            graph.warmupTicks(100).cooldownTicks(50);
        }

        function updateDistance(val) {
            if (!graph) return;
            document.getElementById('distance-value').textContent = val;
            graph.d3Force('link', d3.forceLink().distance(50 * parseFloat(val)));
            graph.warmupTicks(100).cooldownTicks(50);
        }

        function randomizePositions() {
            if (!graph || !graphData) return;
            
            const radius = 100;
            graphData.nodes.forEach((node) => {
                const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
                const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
                
                node.x = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);
                node.y = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta) * 0.5;
                node.z = radius * Math.cos(phi);
            });
            
            graph.graphData(graphData);
            graph.warmupTicks(200).cooldownTicks(100);
        }

        function resetToDefault() {
            document.getElementById('link-width-slider').value = '0.25';
            document.getElementById('link-opacity-slider').value = '0.15';
            document.getElementById('charge-slider').value = '-120';
            document.getElementById('distance-slider').value = '1.0';
            
            updateLinkWidth(0.25);
            updateLinkOpacity(0.15);
            updateCharge(-120);
            updateDistance(1.0);
            
            resetGroupColors();
        }

        function initColorControls() {
            const container = document.getElementById('color-controls');
            if (!container || !graphData) return;
            
            const groups = [...new Set(graphData.nodes.map(n => n.group))];
            let html = '';
            
            groups.forEach(group => {
                const color = colors[group] || '#888888';
                html += `
                    <div class="color-input">
                        <span>${group}:</span>
                        <input type="color" value="${color}" onchange="updateGroupColor('${group}', this.value)">
                    </div>
                `;
            });
            
            container.innerHTML = html;
        }

        function updateGroupColor(group, color) {
            colors[group] = color;
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => {
                    if (n.group === group) return color;
                    return getNodeColor(n.group);
                });
            }
        }

        function resetGroupColors() {
            Object.assign(colors, {
                'Person': '#3366ff',
                'Technology': '#33cc33',
                'Component': '#ff9933',
                'Task': '#ffcc00',
                'Decision': '#cc33ff',
                'Chunk': '#666666'
            });
            
            initColorControls();
            
            if (graph) {
                graph.nodeColor(n => getNodeColor(n.group));
            }
        }

        // Подключаем ползунки после загрузки
        setTimeout(() => {
            document.getElementById('link-width-slider')?.addEventListener('input', (e) => updateLinkWidth(e.target.value));
            document.getElementById('link-opacity-slider')?.addEventListener('input', (e) => updateLinkOpacity(e.target.value));
            document.getElementById('charge-slider')?.addEventListener('input', (e) => updateCharge(e.target.value));
            document.getElementById('distance-slider')?.addEventListener('input', (e) => updateDistance(e.target.value));
        }, 2000);
    </script>
</body>
</html>

## 🤖 **DeepSeek**

**Понял!** Физика была правильная в этой версии. Давайте возьмем параметры физики из неё:

Из старой версии вижу ключевые настройки физики:

```javascript
// Сила отталкивания
.d3Force('charge', d3.forceManyBody().strength(-120))

// Длина связей
.d3Force('link', d3.forceLink().distance(50))

// И возможность регулировки через ползунки:
// charge: от -300 до -30
// distance: от 0.5 до 2 (множитель)
```

Давайте применим эти параметры к нашей текущей рабочей версии:

```bash
cd /Users/kirillkravcov/UniversalCreativeHub/dashboard/public && \

# Сначала убедимся, что у нас рабочая версия
cp blender_hybrid.html.WORKING blender_hybrid.html && \

# Добавляем правильные параметры физики в graphCore.js
cat > js/graphCore.js << 'EOF'
console.log("%c🚀 graphCore.js WITH WORKING PHYSICS", "font-size: 20px; color: #4CAF50; font-weight: bold");

window.GraphCore = {
    graph: null,
    data: { nodes: [], links: [] },
    
    init: function(container) {
        this.container = container;
        this.loadData();
    },
    
    createGraph: function() {
        this.graph = ForceGraph3D()(this.container)
            .graphData(this.data)
            .nodeId('id')
            .nodeLabel('name')
            .nodeColor(n => window.ColorManager ? window.ColorManager.getColor(n.group) : '#888')
            .nodeVal('val')
            .linkWidth(0.65)
            .linkOpacity(0.45)
            .backgroundColor('#0a0a1a')
            .onNodeHover(node => this.onNodeHover(node))
            .onNodeClick(node => this.onNodeClick(node))
            .onBackgroundClick(() => this.onBackgroundClick());
        
        // ПРАВИЛЬНАЯ ФИЗИКА из рабочей версии
        this.graph.d3Force('charge', d3.forceManyBody().strength(-120));
        this.graph.d3Force('link', d3.forceLink().distance(50));
        this.graph.d3VelocityDecay(0.3);
        
        this.graph.cameraPosition({ x: 220, y: 120, z: 300 });
    },
    
    loadData: async function() {
        try {
            const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8004/graph/decisions?limit=500');
            const data = await res.json();
            
            const nodes = data.nodes.map(node => ({
                id: String(node.id),
                name: node.name || node.label || node.id,
                group: node.label || 'unknown',
                val: 3
            }));
            
            const links = data.links ? data.links.map(edge => ({
                source: String(edge.source),
                target: String(edge.target)
            })) : [];
            
            this.data = { nodes, links };
            this.createGraph();
            
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Ошибка:", e);
        }
    },
    
    onNodeHover: function(node) {
        document.body.style.cursor = node ? 'pointer' : 'default';
    },
    
    onNodeClick: function(node) {
        if (window.PanelManager) {
            window.PanelManager.showNodeInfo(node);
        }
    },
    
    onBackgroundClick: function() {
        if (window.PanelManager) {
            window.PanelManager.hideNodePanel();
        }
    }
};

document.addEventListener
